Render only.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:21Z BACKFILL#153 trap.noted trap:a-weaker-blast-that-leaves-the-first-cover v0.1.0: A weaker blast that leaves the first cover alive springs the whole deck, so a stronger blast yields less deck collapse.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#266 trap.noted trap:the-tanks-fill-all-and-tank-fill-to v0.1.0: The 'Tanks: fill ALL' and 'Tank: fill to max' debug-action pickers are UNMEASURED/HARNESS because execute_debug_action's FloatMenu closes before it can be read, so TANK A1 is only a partial pass.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#268 trap.noted trap:the-failing-run-fell-inside-host-fault-window v0.1.0: The failing run fell inside host fault window F20261106T012525Z, so the FAIL may be a harness artefact; it must be confirmed on the clean re-run J20261106T094226Z-e22349 before fixing.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#269 finding.open finding:INDUSTRIAL_RUINS_FIX_HIDDEN_EH_DEPENDENCY v0.1.0: All four industrial ruin scatters in RM_LiquidWorksScatter.xml are MayRequire mandrake.rm.environmentalhazards and About.xml declares no such dependency; with industrialWorksBuildAnywhere=false, FlowWorks without EH generates no industrial ruins, so no industrial works.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#270 trap.noted trap:the-pack-ships-eh-so-the-missing-industrial v0.1.0: The pack ships EH, so the missing industrial works only appears standalone (FlowWorks without EH); Patches/Machinery/RM_LiquidWorksScatter_Register.xml also gates registration on EH.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#271 finding.open finding:JOINED_WEIR_BREACH_FIX_CATCH_UNTOUCHED v0.1.0: Catch is deposited at RunHead().BankCell (RM_Weir.cs:362,385) but Breach->WashCatch washes this.HeldCatch() of the breaching section (RM_Weir.cs:424,459), so a breach away from the head leaves the head's shared catch on the bank.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#272 finding.open finding:LADDER_PARITY_FIX_RAIDERS_SKIP_LEANING v0.1.0: RM_LadderParity.cs MightClimb (267-278) admits only the player faction or a ladder carrier, so a ladderless hostile never reaches CanClimbAt for an already leaning ladder; the RM_WallClimb.cs callers (172,335,367,401,477) reject it and it bashes or waits outside.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#273 finding.open finding:LAKE_FLOOR_SELFTEST_FIX_WET_SKIPS_HEIGHT v0.1.0: LakeFloorChecks.cs EdgeReachable (lines 85,97) returns reachable on any wet cell or wet neighbour before comparing heights, so a sunken lake can be called reachable and the drained-lake access proof misses an inaccessible final descent.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:27Z BACKFILL#274 finding.open finding:LAKE_FLOOR_STAGE_TAME_QUADRATIC_SETS v0.1.0: lake_floor_stage.py:114 builds set(cells) inside the 9-neighbour lambda for every candidate, about 9N^2 insertions (roughly 900 million for a 10,000-cell lake), making natural-lake staging slow.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#275 finding.open finding:LAVA_LIQUID_BUILD_PROMISED_BY_CARD v0.1.0: The lava liquid the owner promoted by card 2026-09-24 was missing from the FlowWorks liquid registry.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#276 finding.verified finding:LAVA_LIQUID_BUILD_PROMISED_BY_CARD v0.1.0: Built at 3f6213543d1f (pre-landing; landed as aab0c1ce14ac) with the lava row in the registry; unverified: A1 selftest_lava_row.py ALL PASS (registry row, Odyssey guard, LavaShallow/LavaDeep, no pump/bottle/world/hot faces, pumping-station exclusion
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#277 finding.open finding:LEVELLING_BUDGET_FIX_STARVES_LATER_HOSES v0.1.0: RM_Levelling.DugBody adds depths.Count to walked (line 324) before the keyIndex cache check (326), and hoses iterate in fixed order, so WalkBudget is spent re-walking known bodies and later hose components are never resolved, every cycle.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#278 trap.noted trap:the-rotating-hose-cursor-applies-only-after-links v0.1.0: The rotating hose cursor applies only after links are constructed, so it cannot rescue the links omitted by budget starvation.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#279 finding.open finding:LEVEL_SET_SELFTEST_FIX_IDLE_PULSE_SETTLES v0.1.0: LevelSetChecks.Run (37-41) records settled as the last changed pulse, so a move/idle oscillation returns 299 of 300 rather than -1, and callers accepting any >=0 (e.g. LowVolumeWideBasinOnePatch restA) report settling.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#280 finding.open finding:LEVEL_WATER_FIX_POND_SPLITS_ARMS v0.1.0: level_water.surface_gaps (578-592) adds a touched pond's surface but never continues the walk through it, so canal arms joined via a pond are scored as separate bodies and a gap violating the 1 cm bar is under-reported (0.0075 vs 0.015 m).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#281 trap.noted trap:the-flaw-is-in-the-python-diagnostic-reference v0.1.0: The flaw is in the Python diagnostic/reference only; equivalence of the production diagnostic was not assumed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#282 finding.open finding:LIQUID_CONVERTER_FIX_PICKS_CRUDE_FIRST v0.1.0: RM_LiquidDefRegistry.xml lists Crude distill before Industrial desalinate (93-108) and toxic equivalents (242-257), and CompLiquidConverter.StepFor (115-133) returns the first eligible conversion, so industrial recipes never run on desal and detox plants.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#283 finding.claimed_fixed finding:LIQUID_CONVERTER_FIX_PICKS_CRUDE_FIRST v0.1.0: Conversions were reordered highest tier first, so the industrial recipe is picked over the crude one for salt-water desal and toxic-water detox. (recorded on pre-landing bd36286c4795; landed as 015b7892e833)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#284 finding.open finding:LIQUID_LEDGER_BUILD_NO_UNIT_LOST v0.1.0: FlowWorks liquid networks needed one transfer service with conservation fixes, components per topology revision, and fill/draw order with split levels (L1-L11) per the semantics design rev 2.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#285 finding.verified finding:LIQUID_LEDGER_BUILD_NO_UNIT_LOST v0.1.0: Built offline L1-L11 (a341da28fa); live passes: lake->pump->Outflow->canal fill, Dump drain, ship tank fill/empty, spill and cleaning (filth 6->0 in 750 ticks).; verified: steve-targeted; unverified: A1 C# SelfTest 267/267 + hose 342/342 + 16/16 FlowWorks python selftests at a341da28fa.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#286 cause.refuted cause:l5-colonists-clean-spill-unmeasured-on-run-20261018t233414 v0.1.0: L5_colonists_clean_spill UNMEASURED on run 20261018T233414 was a harness fault: the precondition demanded Cleaning priority readBack 1 but vanilla stores 3 with manual priorities off; filth went 9 -> 0 in 750 ticks.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#287 trap.noted trap:l4-ship-tank-row-used-a-stand-in v0.1.0: L4 ship-tank row used a stand-in RM_UniversalCargoTank on a quicktest map, not a real gravship; no gravship fixture exists (SITE). L6 goodwill needs --theft-day.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#288 finding.open finding:LIQUID_PUMP_REDRAW_PLAN_VIEW_HUB v0.1.0: RM_LiquidPump and every PumpLook_* draw the pump as a face-on plan-view medallion beside oblique tanks and works, so in game it reads as a flat disc on the ground.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#289 trap.noted trap:the-modern-look-s-red-gloss-is-a v0.1.0: The Modern look's red gloss is a separate item (MODERN_PUMP_REDRAW_SAME_RED_DIFFERENT_DAY); this item is only the camera. No art was regenerated or installed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#290 finding.open finding:MODCHECK_SUITE_CORRECTIONS_1 v0.1.0: First live modcheck wave (min16): 2 GREEN, 12 RED, 2 aborted; most REDs are suite assumption bugs, not mod defects. Suites must be corrected against captured evidence.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#291 trap.noted trap:antiquities-and-shipmemory-s-wait-ticks-failures-were v0.1.0: Antiquities' and ShipMemory's wait_ticks failures were not mod faults: the shared wait_ticks made one unverified step_game_ticks call, and that tool silently truncates (600-2800 ticks/call).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#292 trap.noted trap:rimproperty-s-chain-setup-abort-and-half-of v0.1.0: RimProperty's chain-setup abort and half of Aftermath's 0/3 were not mod faults: the suites assumed Pirate FactionDef guarantees a live Faction, but Biotech's PirateWaster pre-empts vanilla Pirate at worldgen.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#293 trap.noted trap:the-run-log-transient-modcheck-wave-bridge-20260913 v0.1.0: The run log Transient/modcheck_wave_bridge_20260913.log is gone from disk; last copy is in git at 9da13212e8.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:28Z BACKFILL#294 finding.open finding:MODERN_PUMP_REDRAW_SAME_RED_DIFFERENT_DAY v0.1.0: Modern FlowWorks pump, sprinkler head, containers, hose ends and joins are the same red repaint of the Jawa/Industrial shapes rather than real modern products.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#295 trap.noted trap:drafts-are-registered-as-art-ledger-variants-but v0.1.0: Drafts are registered as art-ledger variants but NOT installed (they replace kept art); the pump candidate is tracked under MODERN_LOOK_REDESIGN_BEYOND_RED_ENAMEL.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#296 finding.open finding:MODERN_RED_LEFTOVERS_REDRAW_TODAYS_LOOK v0.1.0: 21 of 169 Modern PNGs under FlowWorks Textures are still 10% or more bright red (13 SuspensionBridge, 4 Hose reel, 2 Rivers bank stake, RM_Bottle_m, RM_Bucket_m).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#297 trap.noted trap:bottle-m-and-bucket-m-are-also-listed v0.1.0: Bottle_m and Bucket_m are also listed on MODERN_PUMP_REDRAW_SAME_RED_DIFFERENT_DAY; do not draw twice. The pump itself belongs to that item.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#298 trap.noted trap:prompt-sources-stopped-asking-for-red-on-2026 v0.1.0: Prompt sources stopped asking for red on 2026-11-06, but art already drawn from them was not redrawn.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#299 finding.open finding:NATURAL_HEAT_CACHE_PROVE_STALE_DAYS v0.1.0: The natural-heat cache trusts terrain for 15,000 ticks after it changes, so a terrain change (e.g. draining a hot pool) is not seen until the cache expires.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#300 finding.verified finding:NATURAL_HEAT_CACHE_PROVE_STALE_DAYS v0.1.0: Built at 5cc4357f960c (pre-landing; landed as 1ba8299de0db): a non-excavated hot/cold kind change marks the natural heat list stale through the DoTerrainChangedEffects postfix; offline selftest and winbuild pass.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#301 trap.noted trap:call-site-coverage-commit-12c563a31-is-a-pre v0.1.0: Call-site coverage commit 12c563a31 is a pre-rebase sha not on main; the commit on origin/main is 7e60caa7c.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#302 finding.open finding:NATURAL_LIQUID_FIX_PUMPS_AS_FRESH_WATER v0.1.0: Natural liquids without canalFluid (ToxicWater in RM_LiquidDefRegistry.xml:215) are skipped by RM_FluidIdentity.cs:27 and default to RM_Fluid_Water in RM_LiquidStock.cs:143,232, so a pump on a toxic pond yields fresh water.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#303 finding.claimed_fixed finding:NATURAL_LIQUID_FIX_PUMPS_AS_FRESH_WATER v0.1.0: Fixed for toxic water only (bd36286c4795 (pre-landing; landed as 015b7892e833)); salt, brine, acid, reaction liquor and coloured waters are not fixed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#304 finding.open finding:NORTH_STAR_WALK_AUTHORING_1 v0.1.0: 72 validation walks carry no `## north star` section; 25 of their `subject:` paths name folders that no longer exist after reorg waves, and 3 pairs of walks describe the same PNG, so authoring bars now would be against inaccurate documents.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#305 trap.noted trap:validating-a-north-star-creates-a-refusal-flowworks v0.1.0: Validating a north star creates a refusal: FlowWorks ships zero textures, so validating its 16 bars made it fail the visual floor. Authoring bars for non-visual mods manufactures refusals.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#306 trap.noted trap:editing-the-north-star-section-of-pits-graffiti v0.1.0: Editing the `## north star` section of Pits, Graffiti, WreckedMachines or FlowWorks silently reverts his validation, because the recorded hash covers the whole section including prose.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#307 trap.noted trap:do-not-redraft-drafting-is-already-done-653d9c7d3a v0.1.0: Do NOT redraft: drafting is already done (653d9c7d3a); remaining work is judging, writing accepted lines and re-validating.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#308 trap.noted trap:modcheck-validate-today-binds-only-on-owner-said v0.1.0: `modcheck validate` today binds only on --owner-said, so delegated new bars need MODCHECK_VALIDATE_BUILD_DELEGATED_NEW_BARS first; any edit to a validated section that removes or relaxes a line stays his.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#309 finding.open finding:OUTFLOW_REGRESSION_FIX_HARDCODED_CAP v0.1.0: fw_pond_outflow_regression.py hardcodes CAP=30, but capacity is bed levels x volumePerTile (shallow = 2 notches), so `drained` is inflated and an undrained pond can pass; channel fills are never asserted.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#310 finding.open finding:P5_SITE_FIX_RERUN_DIGS_DEEPER v0.1.0: depth_rebuild_proofs prepare_site repaints terrain but does not reset excavation; dig() adds deepenLevels to the existing cut, so a second --only p5 run on the same map measures deeper geometry and can falsely fail the 152,000 L expectation.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#311 trap.noted trap:the-default-cli-permits-rerunning-only-p5-on v0.1.0: The default CLI permits rerunning --only p5 on the same map, which silently deepens the previous test geometry; a failure on the second run may be a false fail.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#312 finding.open finding:PIT_RAIN_FIX_FRACTIONAL_HEADROOM_FULL v0.1.0: Rain uses whole-notch RoomNotches; a part-filled cell with under one notch of headroom returns 0, so all rain goes to surface overflow instead of filling the excavation, and contributes nothing if sheet overflow is disabled or refused.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#313 finding.open finding:PIT_RAMP_FIX_WALKER_TAKES_FALL v0.1.0: IsFallingArrival judges by NaturalClimbLimitOf with no ramp exemption, and DescentFalls exempts only ladders/gear, so a captured hostile walking down a valid 3-notch ramp takes fall damage.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#314 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_RAMP_FIX_WALKER_TAKES_FALL v0.1.0: Fixed in source (e3b0a513a649 (pre-landing; landed as 8c8bd865f358)) so a walked step down a ramp is no fall; selftest red-then-green.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#315 finding.open finding:PIT_RESCUE_FIX_COUNTS_DOCTOR_OFF v0.1.0: AnyRescuerReaches (RM_PitAccess.cs:165-167) uses WorkTypeIsDisabled, which tests capability, not the Work-tab setting. A colonist with Doctor unticked counts as a rescuer and suppresses the rescue ladder indefinitely though they never take the rescue job.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#316 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_RESCUE_FIX_COUNTS_DOCTOR_OFF v0.1.0: Batch C source fix so only a colonist assigned Doctor counts as a rescuer; selftest went red then green. Live check still owed. (recorded on pre-landing e3b0a513a649; landed as 8c8bd865f358)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#317 finding.open finding:PIT_RESCUE_FIX_UNREACHABLE_SPARE_LADDER v0.1.0: SparePortable (RM_PitAccess.cs:257-268) picks a minified portable ladder without checking reachability. For one in a sealed chamber, TryQueueFor makes an install blueprint nobody can finish, and the pending blueprint stops later attempts building a ladder, so rescue stalls.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#318 finding.open finding:PIT_SHOT_PROOF_FIX_OTHER_TARGET_PASSES v0.1.0: Row P7_shoot_between_7m_pits_refused (depth_rebuild_proofs.py:418-421) passes whenever the picked target differs from eb. If eb became a legal target but the AI chose another hostile (e.g. the trench-control target), the row still passes.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#319 trap.noted trap:p7-pass-only-means-eb-was-not-selected v0.1.0: P7 PASS only means eb was not selected; the successful control shows target selection works, not why eb lost, so another hostile in the scene can produce a false pass.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:29Z BACKFILL#320 finding.open finding:PIT_TRAVEL_SELFTEST_FIX_DOUBLE_COUNT v0.1.0: Selftest PitTravel_every_trap_mask_reach_and_fall_read_asks_it summed two regexes; Src collapses whitespace so both matched both detectors (4 matches). Removing the free-traveller guard from one detector still gave >=2 and passed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#321 finding.verified finding:PIT_TRAVEL_SELFTEST_FIX_DOUBLE_COUNT v0.1.0: Already fixed on origin by 6089353d23: the summed same-line and \s* patterns became one \s* pattern counted once, so a dropped detector now reads 1 < 2.; verified: steve-targeted
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#322 trap.noted trap:the-old-assertion-looked-guarded-but-one-detector v0.1.0: The old assertion looked guarded but one detector matched both regexes (whitespace collapsed by Src), so it stayed green with one guard removed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#323 trap.noted trap:testq-j20261101t220844z-292508-a1-and-j20261101t234711z-d5138b-a1 v0.1.0: testq J20261101T220844Z-292508.a1 and J20261101T234711Z-d5138b.a1 ran inside Steve SIMD fault window F20261101T164245Z; their verdicts must not be counted.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#324 finding.open finding:PLACER_SELFCHECK_FIX_LEAVES_MERGED_ORE v0.1.0: RM_PlacerProof.SelfCheck cleanup (RM_PlacerWorks.cs:708-746) tracks only newly created Thing ids. Ore merged into a pre-existing stack survives, so repeated checks change colony resources and contaminate later live-state measurements.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#325 finding.open finding:POCKET_TRAP_FIX_LADDER_TO_SEALED_POCKET v0.1.0: RM_PocketGrid.TrappedIn (RM_PocketGrid.cs:822) passes a one-hop UsableLadderIn to Trapped, so a ladder onto a shelf that is itself enclosed reads as escape. The upright stranded pawn is not trapped, so TryQueueFor gives no rescue.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#326 finding.open finding:RAIDERS_TRENCH_BUILD_BREACH_AND_JUMP v0.1.0: Raiders facing a trench with no reachable colonist/building target would stall at the lip; vanilla AssaultColony has no cross-trench job. Surface-to-surface across a trench is never one reachability hop (RelativeVeto), so after ladders stand the raider is sent into the trench and vanilla takes over.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#327 finding.verified finding:RAIDERS_TRENCH_BUILD_BREACH_AND_JUMP v0.1.0: Offline build added RM_RaiderTrench.cs, RM_RaiderTrenchMath.cs, duty patch, job defs, own-faction ladder exemption in LadderLetsOut and ClosedLaddersFor, and Mod Settings section.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#328 trap.noted trap:the-jump-pack-verify-ran-with-colonists-boxed v0.1.0: The jump-pack verify ran with colonists boxed out of sight and read state only, no images; it shows jump landing without descent but not a fight.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#329 finding.open finding:RAID_PLANK_FIX_SURFACE_HEIGHT_SUNKEN v0.1.0: RaidPlankFits accepts equal banks at any depth but RM_RaidPlanks.TryLay never sets deckNotches (stays 0), so a plank between sunken banks registers at surface height, giving wrong access, draw height, underpass class and fall distance.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#330 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RAID_PLANK_FIX_SURFACE_HEIGHT_SUNKEN v0.1.0: Fixed in source (e3b0a513a649 (pre-landing; landed as 8c8bd865f358)): raid plank between sunken banks now lies at their depth; selftest went red then green. Live check still owed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#331 finding.open finding:REFUSED_HANDOVER_DRAIN_FIX_PUDDLE_OVERSTAYS v0.1.0: HandOverTakenCells credits a wet square that became a Receiver/Sink to the engine; whatever the channel refuses (full or a different liquid) stays. On dug/lake cells evaporation and soak are 0 and flow passes skip non-HoldsSheet kinds, so the residue may sit forever (unverified).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#332 finding.open finding:REVIEW_MAP_FIX_STATIONS_NO_LEAD_OUT v0.1.0: In review_map, hose lays at stations 63, 66 and 67 are refused with 'no room for the straight lead-out from the nozzle' (layOk false), so no hose shows. At stations 35/36 'Ladder' never reads 'Ladder up'; cause unknown.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#333 finding.open finding:REVIEW_STAGE_FIX_SMALL_MAPS_HELD_GATES v0.1.0: owner_review_stage places bars_* rows off-map on a 250 map (site at origin+150) without checking map size; held-open gates read open=False after water_scenes_walk.gate_open; the bars_trench shooter has no ranged weapon.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#334 trap.noted trap:mod-failing-to-hold-gates-open-ruled-out v0.1.0: Mod failing to hold gates open: ruled out, by hand Hold open plus a colonist ordered in opens the sluice (open=True seals=False) and it stays open after the colonist leaves; the stage's walk is what fails.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#335 trap.noted trap:the-label-truncates-gate-open-s-report-to v0.1.0: The label truncates gate_open's report to 60 chars, hiding its [button pressed|NOT PRESSED] / [walked|NO COLONIST] detail.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:30Z BACKFILL#336 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_CHECK_SHOOTING_RULE_OFF_PICKS_FARTHER_PIT_FOE v0.1.0: With the superdeep shooting rule OFF, the shooter picked 11098 instead of the nearer pit enemy Human11094. It is not known whether the mod fails to restore vanilla targeting or the row's nearest-target expectation is wrong. shooting_rule_on_pit_only read UNMEASURED.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#337 trap.noted trap:the-depth-menu-s-digger-days-estimate-omits v0.1.0: The depth menu's digger-days estimate omits the x4 labour multiplier, so it understates dig time. Defaults: first soil half-metre 12800 work (~7529 ticks, 3.0 h) at MiningSpeed 1.0; rock 12 h.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#338 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_STRANDED_COLONIST_READS_UNTRAPPED_NO_RESCUE v0.1.0: A colonist stranded in a roofed 5x5 pit dug 4 m reads `trapped=False` in ProofClimb (POCKET pocket=True size=25 depth=8), cannot leave, and the stranded-colonist rescue never queues.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#339 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_JUDGE_FLOWWORKS_REVIEW_SAVE_FOUR_LOOK_QUESTIONS v0.1.0: Review save shows looks no bar decides: dry 1-notch canal reads like a low wall; 5 m water bluer than 2 m; dry 0.5 m and 8 m holes draw alike; geyser pit suffix names 'Water' for tar/oil/blood; owner_look_regressions judge predates shadow design.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#340 trap.noted trap:at-sheet-scale-a-colonist-in-a-dry v0.1.0: At sheet scale a colonist in a dry 0.5 m hole and in an 8 m hole draw alike, though state reads say the draw offset follows depth (plot_H_depth_ladder PASS). The PASS is a state read, not a look.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#341 trap.noted trap:the-small-grey-round-thing-on-the-floor v0.1.0: The small grey round thing on the floor of station 49's 2 m ladder pit is presumed a wild animal, unproven: get_cells_info lists no Thing there but does not list pawns.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#342 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_LAND_RALPH_FLOWWORKS_PIT_FEATURES v0.1.0: Ralph's FlowWorks pit features (raider rescue, bedless prison, hung bridge, ladder rigs, one depth drawing, pit-cover sod, pit looks, raid-stall fixes) sit on origin/ralph/flowworks and never reached main.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#343 trap.noted trap:the-branch-s-depth-drawing-sink-numbers-0 v0.1.0: The branch's depth-drawing sink numbers (0.37..1.50 at 0.5..4 m, knee curve, pitSinkPerLevel 0.3) contradict the owner-confirmed sink of 0.05 per half metre on main (23151d18be); take the drawing, never the numbers.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#344 trap.noted trap:the-branch-is-not-a-merge-it-predates v0.1.0: The branch is not a merge: it predates one-number-per-square water, the sink default change and the separate dig-progress landing (d23e9b6a83); settings files differ heavily.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#345 trap.noted trap:the-clone-unlanded-alarm-for-the-kept-clone v0.1.0: The clone_unlanded alarm for the kept clone is a false alarm: its 36 commits are pushed on origin/ralph/flowworks (tip 69c6028177); the checker counts them unlanded because they are on no main-only mirror tip.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#346 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_LIGHT_FUEL_SAP_TERRAIN_FIRES_STARTED_ZERO v0.1.0: With liquidIgnitionEnabled ON, a burning log beside RM_FuelSapShallow for 500 ticks lights no sap cell (firesStarted=0): vanilla FireUtility refuses terrain with 0 Flammability.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#347 trap.noted trap:the-off-control-and-the-corrosion-rows-passed v0.1.0: The OFF control and the corrosion rows passed in the same run (testq J20261113T071831Z-eaaeff, sha fd568d495b); only liquid_ignition_on fails.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#348 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PROVE_FLOWWORKS_TOGGLES_DENSE v0.1.0: Dense live proofs were owed for four FlowWorks toggles (fallDamageEnabled, liquidCorrosionEnabled, liquidIgnitionEnabled, superdeepShootingRuleEnabled) plus ladder_required_off, and a re-run of six standing FAIL/UNMEASURED chains.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#349 trap.noted trap:ingest-skips-exact-tier-proofs-relentless-game-reads v0.1.0: Ingest skips exact-tier proofs (relentless_game reads them), so no verify was recorded for the 2026-11-13 run. Earlier jobs died RmDataRootUnset until RM_DATA_ROOT was kept in WSLENV (fd568d495b).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#350 trap.noted trap:first-real-run-shooting-rule-off-fail-filed v0.1.0: First real run: shooting_rule_off FAIL (filed RIMMANDRAKE_CHECK_SHOOTING_RULE_OFF_PICKS_FARTHER_PIT_FOE), shooting_rule_on_pit_only UNMEASURED upstream, liquid_ignition_on FAIL (filed RIMMANDRAKE_LIGHT_FUEL_SAP_TERRAIN_FIRES_STARTED_ZERO)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:31Z BACKFILL#351 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_RESUME_SWEPT_PAWN_STUCK_MID_RIVER v0.1.0: RM_MapComponent_RiverCurrent.Move() calls pather.StopDead() + Notify_Teleported(false); the pather has no path and the job is not ended, so a Goto toil (completes only on PatherArrival) waits forever and the pawn stands frozen until drafted.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#352 trap.noted trap:the-criss-cross-reservoir-ripples-were-not-mainly v0.1.0: The criss-cross reservoir ripples were not mainly the engine: the compare pages took full steps plus a short tail step (0.19/0.11 s alternating), growing frcap reservoir roughness to 0.18 m at 33 s versus 0.0003 m with equal steps. Equal steps remove it.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#353 trap.noted trap:the-froude-speed-cap-does-not-fix-the v0.1.0: The Froude speed cap does not fix the reservoir ripples (owner told plainly); ripple fixes are deferred to the next version after live viewing.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#354 trap.noted trap:seven-of-eight-new-ripple-dampers-fail-the v0.1.0: Seven of eight new ripple dampers fail the bars: explicit dampers inside the theta/alpha stability margin cut 33 s reservoir roughness only 10-17% (fail 1.3x bar); stronger ones blow the lake at rest.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#355 trap.noted trap:earlier-pit-dig-bakeoff-rows-measured-a-dry v0.1.0: Earlier pit_dig bakeoff rows measured a dry pit because the canal was cut a fixed 2 m into a bank rising away from the river, leaving a ridge; rows were rerun after the scene fix. Pit fill for explicit diffusion and the CG implicit solver is untested because they are too slow for the test.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#356 trap.noted trap:accepted-costs-undying-criss-cross-ripples-after-a v0.1.0: Accepted costs: undying criss-cross ripples after a dam burst, rivers deliver 12% less.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#357 trap.noted trap:the-smoothdiff-fr-1-21x-ms-tick-figure v0.1.0: The smoothdiff_fr 1.21x ms/tick figure is close to its bar: unpaired runs under machine load read 1.24-1.38x. Its C# cost is unmeasured (guess 10-20%).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#358 trap.noted trap:ramp-steps-are-hex-sized-because-one-tile v0.1.0: Ramp steps are hex-sized because one tile draws one colour; a smooth ramp inside a tile needs a postfix recolouring submesh vertices. Visual death is alpha/width fade; an actual river end is a link edit, separate from colour.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#359 trap.noted trap:no-toggle-row-ran-in-20261012g-13b-ext v0.1.0: No toggle row ran in 20261012g/13b ext_river (only 5 chains), so those runs do not prove the toggles. A5 was stale: weir drift rolls were removed (no auto fish capture); it now names the capture and flotsam proofs.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#360 finding.open finding:SESSION_HURT_DIAGNOSE_BRUISED_AT_THE_BELL v0.1.0: Colonists were hurt mid-script (Bruise, Crack, RM_SwimExhaustion), so GovernorStop voided climb_proof, sprinkler_live and liquid_checks L2/L4/L6. park_bystanders walked pawns to map centre across the river, where they swam and were carried downstream.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#361 finding.verified finding:SESSION_HURT_DIAGNOSE_BRUISED_AT_THE_BELL v0.1.0: Start colonists are quenched of RM_Thirst and parked by teleport into a dry 5x5 pen (23ce5f907) instead of walked across the river, with a per-script hediff census (live_session.py).; verified: steve-targeted (recorded on pre-landing 61131dde14fb; landed as 2ad8ba48157c)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#362 cause.refuted cause:spores-were-not-the-cause-of-the-later v0.1.0: Spores were not the cause of the later A1 failure: after the spore purge Vikinger, parked and drafted at 126,126, still ended at 160,95 with Bruise + Crack. Presumed harness (parking not holding a drafted pawn across the sprinkler block), unproven.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#363 trap.noted trap:order-pawn-success-means-arrived-so-with-waitticks v0.1.0: order_pawn success means ARRIVED, so with waitTicks=1 every park read False; park status from the old walking park is not trustworthy.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#364 finding.open finding:SMALL_PUMP_HOSE_FIX_LAYS_UNCONNECTED v0.1.0: Targeting an isolated RM_LiquidPump (1x1, PassThroughOnly) leaves the end on the pump cell: ApproachIfOnPort returns early because the cell is Walkable (CompHoseReel.cs:415), SideOf(0,0)=-1 (HosePorts.cs:158-160), so no coupling. The order succeeds but the hose stays disconnected.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#365 finding.open finding:SUNKEN_DRAWBRIDGE_FIX_REJECTS_HINGE_BANK v0.1.0: Raising a sunken drawbridge starts the landing search on its hinge anchors, but passable (RM_Drawbridges.cs:475-486) rejects ClimbDepthAt>0 cells unless decked. Every start is rejected, so no bank search occurs and riders become overflow and fall.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:32Z BACKFILL#366 finding.open finding:SUSPENSION_BASE_REWIRE_COVERS_STEP_OUT v0.1.0: Task: lift Building_PitCover into a span base class, with pit covers as its hidden trap tier, and replace 'is covered' at seven call sites with one RM_SpanUtility.DeckedAt(map, c, out deckHeightM) read. No behaviour change.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#367 trap.noted trap:the-earlier-live-fail-120-kg-held-121 v0.1.0: The earlier live FAIL (120 kg held 121.0 kg, 220 kg held 244.3 kg) was not a mod defect: the cover rows called set_draft without drafted=True, so colonists stayed undrafted and wandered off the deck. The mod read clean.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#368 trap.noted trap:the-2026-10-19-a3-fail-verify-rerun v0.1.0: The 2026-10-19 A3 fail verify (rerun_20261019T010244) is a harness artefact and must be disregarded; a pawn off its deck cell is now UNMEASURED.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#369 finding.open finding:SWALE_CANAL_ART_REFERENCE_1 v0.1.0: RM_Swale art is a placeholder; real FlowWorks canal screenshots are needed to spec it. Its v2 render did not read like the live canal, which is flat blue-grey water with no bank art.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#370 finding.open finding:SWIM_WARNING_FIX_ANY_PAWN_MESSAGE v0.1.0: depth_rebuild_proofs.py:233-244: SWIM_armour_warned_before_under counts any new swim/drown message from any pawn, and accepts warned<=under in the same sample. A drowning announcement passes it even if the early tiring warning is broken.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#371 trap.noted trap:swim-armour-warned-before-under-can-pass-green v0.1.0: SWIM_armour_warned_before_under can pass green while the early tiring warning is broken, since the drowning message matches the filter in the same sample.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#372 finding.open finding:TAKEN_BY_LAND_SERVICE_1 v0.1.0: The gale carry and FlowWorks' swept-away are the same hold, letter, return mechanism built twice, and the spot where a pawn was taken has no ground trace, so pawns vanish without a readable sign.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#373 trap.noted trap:runs-of-j20261101t070105z-adb7ec-j20261101t220844z-292508-and-j20261101t234711z v0.1.0: Runs of J20261101T070105Z-adb7ec, J20261101T220844Z-292508 and J20261101T234711Z-d5138b ran inside Steve SIMD fault windows and are quarantined; their verdicts must not be counted.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#374 trap.noted trap:several-testq-runs-recorded-no-verdict-refused-sync v0.1.0: Several testq runs recorded no verdict (REFUSED/SYNC, INVALID/HARNESS, INVALID/LOAD, INVALID/DEPLOY with a stale companion DLL), so they are not passes.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#375 finding.open finding:TAR_COOLING_GLASS_MEND_ENGINE_CELLS v0.1.0: Tar cooling writes glass onto cells the depth engine owns (Flood_FlowWorks.cs ~377-390), overwriting excavated front cells' depth terrain.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#376 finding.verified finding:TAR_COOLING_GLASS_MEND_ENGINE_CELLS v0.1.0: Tar release cooling now skips excavated front cells (ae21b56dbb89 (pre-landing; landed as 222bdcfa794c)); offline selftest passes, live A2 still owed.; verified: A1 L1 PASS (offline-selftest+winbuild); unverified: A2 live: a tar release into a dug channel leaves its base depth terrain intact after it finishes
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#377 finding.open finding:TAR_LAG_FIX_NO_WATER_CONTROL v0.1.0: The tar lag check (_expect_tar_lag, extensions.py:746-753) asserts only 1<=wet<len(cells) with no water control. A regression to ordinary one-cell-per-pulse speed leaves 6 of 10 cells wet after six pulses and still passes.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#378 trap.noted trap:the-tar-viscosity-check-reads-as-proving-tar v0.1.0: The tar viscosity check reads as proving tar lags water, but 'not all ten cells wet' no longer distinguishes viscosity from normal front pacing, since the water-front check expects gradual propagation.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#379 finding.open finding:TAR_REFINERY_FIX_CAN_FALLBACK_DEAD v0.1.0: The RM_Liquid_Tar refine row has both product and productThing. CompLiquidConverter.cs:239-241 uses tank output whenever product is non-null, so the can fallback the refinery descriptions promise never runs; the refinery stops without an output tank.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#380 finding.claimed_fixed finding:TAR_REFINERY_FIX_CAN_FALLBACK_DEAD v0.1.0: Three tar refinery descriptions no longer promise cans beside the machine, and the productThing was removed from the RM_Liquid_Tar refine row in registry and generator, because code doc 4a34f746d4 rules items are never a fallback for a full tank.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#381 trap.noted trap:the-ticket-says-not-fixed-but-the-implemented v0.1.0: The ticket says 'Not fixed' but the implemented event (705e7935e178) fixed it by changing the descriptions, not by making the can fallback work.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#382 finding.open finding:TIMBER_WINCH_FIX_POWERS_STEEL_BRIDGE v0.1.0: A winch links to a drawbridge by adjacency only (RM_Drawbridges.cs:699-703), no tier match; a timber winch reports Powered with no power comp (RM_DrawbridgeControl.cs:44-45), so a steel leaf moves unpowered.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#383 finding.open finding:TRANSFER_AUDIT_FIX_FAILED_MOVE_COUNTS v0.1.0: TransferChecks.cs:551 regex `==\s*\w` also matches `== 0` (nothing committed), so CommittedBlocks/Lint accepts a credit inside a failed-Move branch. Latent: no such site today.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#384 finding.open finding:UNDERPASS_SHOT_FIX_OVERRIDES_VANILLA_REJECT v0.1.0: RM_Patch_SuspensionBandShots.Postfix (RM_SuspensionPatches.cs:752-768) replaces a false CanHitTargetFrom result with only the cliff test when both ends are floor-side, so a wall-blocked shot under a deck becomes legal.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#385 finding.claimed_fixed finding:UNDERPASS_SHOT_FIX_OVERRIDES_VANILLA_REJECT v0.1.0: Batch C fix in source (e3b0a513a649 (pre-landing; landed as 8c8bd865f358)): the underpass shot postfix reopens only a cliff rejection and re-asks vanilla; selftest red-then-green. Live check owed.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#386 finding.open finding:VV_MATRIX_FIX_SURFACE_FAILURES_UNDERCOUNT v0.1.0: ShippedMatrixProbe.cs:165 sets failures = Math.Max(a,b,c), a lower bound exported as a count, so differing failing scenes are understated in the JSON/console rates.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#387 finding.open finding:WALKS3_OUTFLOW_FIX_HOSE_WONT_JUMP_DITCH v0.1.0: water_scenes_walk s3_geom() sets outflow=canal[0] and _pump_rig lays hose B onto that dug depth-1 square; CordWorld.StepAllowed refuses the band change without a ramp/ladder link, so the lay reads "no route" with line-crossing rules on.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:33Z BACKFILL#388 trap.noted trap:a-bank-end-s3-outflow-bank-used-by v0.1.0: A bank end (S3["outflow_bank"], used by sitting_gaps_proof) may not be enough, since S3.a/S3.b need the outflow to pour INTO the canal.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#389 finding.open finding:WEIR_CATCH_FIX_ESCAPES_CAP_RADIUS v0.1.0: GenPlace Near placement of a captured stack (RM_Weir.cs:385) is unbounded, while HeldCatch counts only within HeldCatchRadius 2.5 (line 123), so displaced stacks are missed by the cap and by the breach wash.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#390 finding.open finding:BOTTLE_STOCK_MEND_FREE_REFILLS v0.1.0: Bottle/bucket/barrel fill (JobDriver_FillBottle, RM_LiquidBottleUtility.TryGetLiquidAt reads terrain only) and wash (JobDriver_WashBottle) never debit the body's RM_LiquidStock, so a limited pond creates liquid. MakeContainer builds a fresh Thing without HitPoints, healing containers.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#391 finding.open finding:CANAL_SWIM_VERIFY_POISON_POOL_PARTY v0.1.0: Poison/oil/fuel canal fills keep the Water tag and have no no-swim marker; JoyGiver_GoSwimming needs only IsWater+Standable, so colonists may swim recreationally in them.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#392 trap.noted trap:propane-was-not-wired-here-it-was-split v0.1.0: Propane was not wired here; it was split out as PROPANE_CANAL_WIRE_STILL_NO_FLUID.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#393 finding.open finding:COVER_DECK_TUNE_QUADRATIC_CENSUS v0.1.0: Every pit cover floods its whole deck each scan (O(N^2) on big covered moats; CompPitCoverTrigger.cs:73-76 builds Deck before the lead check). A mixed-tier deck springs at the lowest-id lead's tier, and decks over 400 covers are truncated (Building_PitCover.cs:65), giving several leads and partial collapse.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#394 finding.open finding:DBH_WATER_MEND_ACID_ON_TAP v0.1.0: 19 hazardous liquid terrains (acid, propane, tar, slime, poisoned water) inherit the dbh_water tag, so with DBH they become wash/drink sources. DBH integration also fires only for 'Dubs Bad Hygiene Lite', not full DBH, and a bucket's DBH water value is fixed at 5.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#395 trap.noted trap:bucket-dbh-water-value-stays-fixed-at-5 v0.1.0: Bucket DBH water value stays fixed at 5 while material scales its contents (metal 6, leather 4); WaterExt is per ThingDef, so it cannot be fixed in XML. Offline selftest only, no live proof run.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#396 finding.open finding:DRILL_SURVEY_EXPLAIN_BEFORE_STEEL_IS_SPENT v0.1.0: The subsurface survey ('nothing drillable found under this map') was readable only on a built drill (Building_LiquidDrill.GetInspectString), so a player spent steel, components and 400 W to learn nothing was there.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#397 finding.claimed_fixed finding:DRILL_SURVEY_EXPLAIN_BEFORE_STEEL_IS_SPENT v0.1.0: The drill/tap PlaceWorker now draws the survey beside the mouse while placing ('Subsurface: <liquid>, <litres> remaining', or red 'nothing drillable under this map / reserve used up'), plus a red line when drilling is off. Drill description updated. Placement not refused. (recorded on pre-landing 4e090d7ba1
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#398 finding.open finding:FERRY_ROPE_FIX_BUILT_ONLY_WHEN_ASKED v0.1.0: Ferry rope cells are derived and built only when something reads them (EnsureWorks via InPool/OnRope/RopeCellCount), so undrafted routing (ApplyRope reads RopeIndicesNoRebuild) can ignore a valid ferry after load or pairing. Posts also pair with the nearest unpaired post even on the same bank.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#399 trap.noted trap:the-r9-ferry-proof-called-ropecellcount-first-which v0.1.0: The r9 ferry proof called RopeCellCount first, which masks the bug by forcing the rope build.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#400 trap.noted trap:same-bank-pairing-c6-f8-remains-unfixed-telling v0.1.0: Same-bank pairing (C6-F8) remains unfixed: telling banks apart needs a land-connectivity test, deferred as new scope.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:34Z BACKFILL#401 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_DONOR_AFFORDANCE_GAP_1 v0.1.0: FlowWorks' 19 generated liquid TerrainDefs reference donor affordances BMT_DeepWaterBridgeable (x38) and TST_TerrainForMeditationStone (x19), giving 57 crossref errors when the donor mods are absent.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#402 finding.claimed_fixed finding:FLOWWORKS_DONOR_AFFORDANCE_GAP_1 v0.1.0: Added DONOR_AFFORDANCE_MAYREQUIRE in generate_liquid_suite.py to gate both affordances with MayRequire; regenerated 19 TerrainDef files (only header and two <li> lines changed); validated 0 errors/0 warnings. (recorded on pre-landing c3bbfff0bab8; landed as a2d528b935a7)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#403 cause.refuted cause:defining-own-rm-affordances-was-rejected-nothing-in v0.1.0: Defining own RM_ affordances was rejected: nothing in FlowWorks checks these affordances, donor buildings check their own names, so an RM_ substitute would do nothing.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#404 trap.noted trap:only-the-two-named-strings-were-grepped-no v0.1.0: Only the two named strings were grepped; no sweep for other donor cross-references in FlowWorks was done. The dbh_water tag is left alone since tags don't resolve cross-references.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#405 trap.noted trap:the-19-rm-xml-files-are-generated-edit v0.1.0: The 19 RM_*.xml files are GENERATED; edit the generator table, not the files, or the fix regresses on regeneration.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#406 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_DOOR_FAMILY_1 v0.1.0: Task: add two stuffable doors, Sluice (cheap, holds small creatures) and SecurityGrateDoor (holds a real prisoner), both passing liquid while closed, with stuff deciding armour, fire survival and prisoner toughness.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#407 trap.noted trap:a-wooden-sluice-burning-down-is-intended-not v0.1.0: A wooden sluice burning down is intended, not a bug; a grate does not stop fire, burning oil passes through it.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#408 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_FORCED_PIT_FALL_ROW_1 v0.1.0: validation_v2 row P3 asserts only the refusal of walking into a pit (path veto 790fd4e8d, RM_PitTrapMath.cs:244-269), so a forced entry (flyer landing or knockback) into a D=4 pit with no ladder was unmeasured.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#409 finding.verified finding:FLOWWORKS_FORCED_PIT_FALL_ROW_1 v0.1.0: Added P3f_forced_entry_falls and P4f_ladder_frees_forced to validation_v2; they go red under mock faults pit_no_fall and pit_forced_stays_held. Live run GREEN 61/61: flyer into D=4, 1 fall wound, held, walk-in control refused, freed by lowered ladder.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#410 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_HOTPATH_MEASURE_EVERY_FRAME v0.1.0: Hot-path perf: every connected reel builds a new feed Mesh every rendered frame (DrawFeed, RM_MapComponent_Hoses.cs:950-966), a hose carrier rebuilds the whole-map World() snapshot as its trail changes, and bottle work givers repeat a radial search per container.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#411 cause.refuted cause:c1-f6-hose-link-scan-rm-hoselinks-refresh v0.1.0: C1-F6 hose-link scan: RM_HoseLinks.Refresh runs once per map per tick (checkedTick), O(reels), so not a bug.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#412 cause.refuted cause:c7a-f8-corridor-hash-rect-bounded-by-maxlength v0.1.0: C7a-F8 corridor hash: rect bounded by maxLength<=80 and checked once per 250 ticks per laid reel, so not a bug.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#413 cause.refuted cause:c7b-f7-carry-trail-growth-grows-only-while v0.1.0: C7b-F7 carry trail growth: grows only while PulledLength<=MaxLength and revisits truncate; ShortenBy runs only while winding, so a 1600-cell serpentine needs deliberate micro; not a bug.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#414 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_LIQUID_FACES_1 v0.1.0: Task: produce one matrix of every LiquidDef registry liquid/fuel against floods, rain, rivers, lakes, oceans and ground pump, each cell YES (with hosting mod/biome), NO (with why) or LATER, plus a native-liquid declaration per biome. Builds nothing itself.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#415 finding.claimed_fixed finding:FLOWWORKS_LIQUID_FACES_1 v0.1.0: Matrix completed and ruled: 17 liquids x 7 faces, 28/28 native declarations (22 liquid, 6 NONE, 0 OPEN), pump table ruled, 10 owner rulings recorded in design/RimMandrake/flowworks_liquid_matrix.md; downstream biome items carry rulings as notes. (recorded on pre-landing 0161e37f1; landed as f31bd23f832b)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:35Z BACKFILL#416 trap.noted trap:map-edges-are-sinks-never-sources-edge-law v0.1.0: Map edges are SINKS, never sources (edge law). An on-map ocean that is canal-connected would be an infinite source. The frozen world map is not touched: oceans are map-scale and lore faces only until the paint pass.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:38Z BACKFILL#450 cause.refuted cause:s3-was-not-a-classifier-bug-w4-is v0.1.0: S3 was not a classifier bug: W4 is a 40-cell EDGE body, and LAKE_GROUNDWATER_REFILL_BUILD_WETNESS_CUT (392300ef0d) makes every edge body limitless however small, so limitless=True is correct.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:38Z BACKFILL#451 cause.refuted cause:p3f-p4f-were-not-a-mod-regression-proofforcedflyer v0.1.0: P3f/P4f were not a mod regression: ProofForcedFlyer launched ThingDefOf.PawnFlyer (no stun), a deliberate jump that lands safely since 0bed9b5796 (pitJumpLandsSafe, RM_Patch_PawnFlyer_LandInPit).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:38Z BACKFILL#452 trap.noted trap:a-forced-entry-proof-that-launches-plain-pawnflyer v0.1.0: A forced-entry proof that launches plain PawnFlyer is a deliberate jump and lands safely, so it never falls; only PawnFlyer_Stun forces a fall. A stale DLL can also produce a non-Stun launch.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:38Z BACKFILL#453 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_WATERBODY_DIAGNOSE_SOIL_THROW v0.1.0: Laying soil threw 'Sequence contains no matching element' inside vanilla WaterBodyTracker.RemoveCellFromExistingBody after a water cell changed waterBodyType: the old body keeps the cell in CellCount, its rootCell reassignment's First(...) then throws.

Three repros (flip, flip-and-back, 4x1 strip) verified clean live.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:38Z BACKFILL#455 cause.refuted cause:flowworks-rm-patch-liquidheatterrainchanged-was-not-the-cause v0.1.0: FlowWorks' RM_Patch_LiquidHeatTerrainChanged was not the cause: its postfix was only listed on the Harmony stack of DoTerrainChangedEffects and never ran, because the original threw first. It is a vanilla 1.6 Odyssey bug.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#488 finding.open finding:LIQUID_FIRE_MEND_COVER_AND_SAVE v0.1.0: RM_LiquidFire does not save carriedAcc/carriedTick/carriedFluid/burningFluid, so after load TakeCarried treats a null fluid as matching any. ApplyEffects ignores cover and Flying, and blastHops is keyed by centre cell only, so a later explosion inherits the 300-tick hop limit.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#489 trap.noted trap:closed-with-only-the-offline-selftest-gptfixesachecks-no v0.1.0: Closed with only the offline selftest GptFixesAChecks; no live proof was run.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#490 finding.open finding:LIQUID_ROTATION_FIX_STARVED_STRANGER v0.1.0: MayDraw (RM_LiquidNetKernel.cs:447) keys consumer rotation by LiquidDef only, so an isolated starved machine re-starves each cycle and a supplied machine is never served. FirstRunnable always returns the first positive liquid, so multi-input converters never rotate.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#491 trap.noted trap:closed-on-the-offline-selftest-gptfixesachecks-only-no v0.1.0: Closed on the offline selftest GptFixesAChecks only; no live proof run.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#492 finding.open finding:LIQUID_THEFT_FIX_TANK_HIDES_ITSELF v0.1.0: RM_LiquidTheft.Witness uses the 3-arg GenSight.LineOfSight ending at tank.Position; the 2x2 RM_LiquidTank (fillPercent 1.0) blocks sight through its other three cells, so a witness whose line enters elsewhere never sees it and caught stays 0.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#493 finding.verified finding:LIQUID_THEFT_FIX_TANK_HIDES_ITSELF v0.1.0: Witness now aims LineOfSight at the tank's OccupiedRect (rect overload) instead of the single Position cell, so witnesses on any side, including NE, see the 2x2 tank; selftest pins the production call. (recorded on pre-landing ed20705bd5fa; landed as f239f3745335)
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#494 trap.noted trap:live-proof-came-from-a-ne-witness-probe v0.1.0: Live proof came from a NE witness probe (caught 0->1, goodwill 0->-2), not from that row.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#495 trap.noted trap:gss-s-power-tap-twin-is-unaffected-because v0.1.0: GSS's power-tap twin is unaffected because its clamp is 1x1, so it caught on the first cycle; do not copy this fix there.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:41Z BACKFILL#496 finding.open finding:LIQUID_THEFT_VERIFY_FALSE_ACCUSATION v0.1.0: RM_LiquidTheft.TheftTick compares stock snapshots only, so any change on a foreign tank (corrosion, heat, its owners' machines) charges goodwill as player theft; a balanced take+put between cycles escapes.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#498 finding.verified finding:LIQUID_WAKES_FIX_FOOTPRINTS_GONE_QUIET v0.1.0: Live W1 on f74444a695 + b380d127ee: water, slime and tar each produced 3 wakes with the setting on and 0 off, and the setting was restored. The record does not itemise the individual fixes.; verified: A1 L2 PASS (flowworks_live) (recorded on pre-landing f74444a69538; landed as 90a7e94f4c4d)

- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#500 cause.refuted cause:second-run-the-mod-s-wake-logic-was v0.1.0: Second run: the mod's wake logic was not wrong; both causes were HARNESS (MAX_SAMPLES=80 x SAMPLE_TICKS=3 = 240 ticks too short for pathCost 200 slime / 300 tar; water baseline parsed as None from an empty first snap).
- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#501 trap.noted trap:w1-as-written-never-stood-on-any-liquid v0.1.0: W1 as written never stood on any liquid (pawn paths around the 5-row strips, one snapshot after order_pawn), never toggled liquidWakesEnabled or counted wakes, and wanted RM_Fill_Water though dug water now settles to RM_WaterThin; its earlier results said nothing about wakes.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#502 finding.open finding:MAP_STATICS_PRUNE_GHOST_MAPS v0.1.0: Static per-Map tables (RM_LiquidNet dictionaries pruned only on a cache miss, RM_PitPathing NativeArray grids, RM_DigDiscovery poolMap) and RM_LiquidSurface pooled Unity meshes outlive removed maps, pinning memory and stale things.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#503 finding.open finding:PANNING_SPOT_VERIFY_EMPTY_RIVER v0.1.0: Panning keeps being offered after every placer rock is mined (stock > 0, pool empty): ShouldSkip in RM_PlacerWorks.cs:487 reads stock only, while Draw returns null when the pool is empty.
- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#504 finding.open finding:PIPE_ADAPTER_FIX_DEBT_NEVER_PAID v0.1.0: Adapter Send mode with a partial foreign accept: SettleSend refunds the whole unit and records -fraction in carry, but the Send path never consults negative carry (only Take does), so liquid is created.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:42Z BACKFILL#507 finding.open finding:PIT_FALL_MEND_SAVED_MIDAIR v0.1.0: A save in the same tick as a jump/forced fall loses the fall damage: RM_SuperdeepTrapState.lastCell is derived, never scribed, and TryJumpInto teleports and defers the descent to the next detector pass.

- 2026-11-16T11:41:43Z BACKFILL#511 finding.open finding:PUMP_POUR_TUNE_NO_FASTER_THAN_GRAVITY v0.1.0: Basic RM_LiquidPump on Pour drains a Dump tank no faster than gravity: levelsPerCycle defaults to 1 (5 units/250-tick cycle), equal to dumpUnitsPerCycle (5). Live 20 vs 25 units per 1000 ticks, against ruling Q3 that pour is the fast drain.

CanReachMapEdge is not vetoed for a surface island ringed by an unclimbable trench. Absolute-rule trap BFS walks through walls.

- 2026-11-16T11:42:28Z BACKFILL#933 cause.refuted cause:the-route-planner-entering-wall-cells-or-a v0.1.0: The route planner entering wall cells (or a stale lay) is not the cause: RouteCells/CordPlanner walk only walkable cells, and a fresh re-lay repeats the fault; the cause was per-cell Clear depth.

- 2026-11-16T11:42:28Z BACKFILL#935 cause.refuted cause:the-first-build-made-pipes-join-only-on v0.1.0: The first build made pipes join only on a side or under, never at a corner; the owner ruled this wrong: pipes join whenever adjacent, corners included.

Measured offline, not in game.

- 2026-11-16T15:47:32Z JUDGE.judge-1794833810#4 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_FLOWWORKS_THIRST_CALLER_FALL_UNSEEN v0.1.0: T_THIRST proves the ruled fall numbers in RM_ThirstMath but not that Need_RM_Thirst uses them: planted FallPerInterval(BaseFallPerDay) (no heat x3, labour, sleep or rate slider) left T_THIRST green.

- 2026-11-19T23:57:35Z BACKFILL#985 trap.noted trap:assumptions-a1-a5-are-provisional-such-as-d3 v0.1.0: Assumptions A1-A5 are PROVISIONAL, such as D3 face height of about 0.25 cell and the 20-day scorch fade; the owner judges on the sheet/map.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:39Z BACKFILL#986 trap.noted trap:the-regression-can-pass-with-no-drainage-six v0.1.0: The regression can pass with no drainage: six shallow cells hold 12 notch units, not 30, so an untouched pond reports drained=18, above the 6.5 threshold; if the inlet remains a source every scored condition passes.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:40Z BACKFILL#987 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_FLOWWORKS_FUZZ_FOUR_FLAWS_SINK_STILL_FLOAT v0.1.0: Fuzz test machinery flaws (static review, not reproduced): per-fluid and sink laws compare float levels at 1e-3 (10 dL), so a 1 dL change passes; probe failure counts are a lower bound; planted counts include earlier families; JSON writer leaves chars below U+0020 unescaped.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:40Z BACKFILL#988 trap.noted trap:the-findings-are-a-static-review-not-reproduced v0.1.0: The findings are a static review, not reproduced by the filer; line numbers are those of dbf76d554c. Only test machinery is affected, not the mod.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:40Z BACKFILL#989 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_JAWABENCH_AWARENESS_INSTALL_CRASHES_START v0.1.0: The game crashes natively at startup on some launches. Two crashes follow the JawaBench awareness line, in the Install lambda via TryCreateInstance; a third came earlier. The first up after deploy crashed 3 of 3, restarts 4 of 4 clean.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:40Z BACKFILL#990 trap.noted trap:the-awareness-frame-is-not-the-only-crash v0.1.0: The awareness frame is not the only crash site: a third crash came right after the FlowWorks hose essential patches, and a mid-run native crash ~7 min into a fifth fresh map. Whether first-up-after-deploy matters is unproven.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:40Z BACKFILL#991 trap.noted trap:a-fresh-quicktest-map-once-came-up-with v0.1.0: A fresh quicktest map once came up with Verse.MapDrawer.get_SectionCount NRE in FinalizeInit (every bridge batch op NREs); a restart cleared it.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:40Z BACKFILL#992 trap.noted trap:logs-are-overwritten-by-the-next-launch-crash v0.1.0: Logs are overwritten by the next launch; crash reports are under the Crashes temp folder.

- 2026-11-19T23:57:41Z BACKFILL#994 trap.noted trap:a-fourth-impl-value-partial-in-the-schema v0.1.0: A fourth impl value 'partial' in the schema was rejected: every reader of impl and the coverage rules would need a rule for half-built; impl answers only 'is there code for it', how much works is the proof field.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:41Z BACKFILL#995 trap.noted trap:teaching-showcases-alone-to-read-partial-was-rejected v0.1.0: Teaching 'showcases' alone to read partial was rejected: it would create a second vocabulary.

- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1043 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PLANT_FLOWWORKS_SETTLING_LAW_SHUTTLE_TRIPS_OTHERS v0.1.0: The settling law settled_scene_reaches_fixed_point was never seen red: the ShippedMatrixProbe shuttle plant turned kernel_fuzz red only via fluids_never_turn_into_each_other or sink_drain_accounted_cell_by_cell, because per-pulse checks return first and the probe hook cannot see fluid or sinks.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1044 finding.verified finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PLANT_FLOWWORKS_SETTLING_LAW_SHUTTLE_TRIPS_OTHERS v0.1.0: Shuttle plant now keeps one pair and moves 1 dL, tripping no other law; the 24-pulse window runs per-pulse checks and counts no filling body. settling/kernel_fuzz PROVEN red 13/10000 by settled_scene_reaches_fixed_point, baseline 0/10000.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1045 cause.refuted cause:the-original-shuttle-was-assumed-to-reverse-or v0.1.0: The original shuttle was assumed to reverse or continue: it need not, since with depths [2,2] and water [U,U] the first move leaves [2U,0] and neither cell is eligible again; the pair is not kept between calls and need not be adjacent (Codex finding 3).
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1046 trap.noted trap:settling-proves-on-10000-scenes-because-only-58 v0.1.0: Settling proves on 10000 scenes because only 58/10000 are settled-expected; a smaller run may not reach the law. Codex's remaining per-fluid/sink/count findings stay open in RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_FLOWWORKS_FUZZ_FOUR_FLAWS_SINK_STILL_FLOAT.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1047 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_SHARPEN_FLOWWORKS_VOLUME_LAW_ONE_DECILITRE_SLIPS_BY v0.1.0: The FlowWorks conservation law (volume_in_equals_out_plus_stored, FlowKernelFuzz.cs) did not see 1 dL appearing from nowhere: its float tolerance of 1e-3 levels equals 10 dL (U = 10000 dL/level), so a sub-level leak passed (0/300 red over 1156 plants).
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1048 finding.verified finding:RIMMANDRAKE_SHARPEN_FLOWWORKS_VOLUME_LAW_ONE_DECILITRE_SLIPS_BY v0.1.0: The law now compares exactly in dL instead of the 10 dL float tolerance, and the probe plants one dL. Prove on base NOT_PROVEN (0/300, planted 1156); after PROVEN (baseline 0/300, red 162/300); unplanted 10000 scenes 0 failures.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1049 cause.refuted cause:the-kernel-does-not-round-the-decilitre-away v0.1.0: The kernel re-rounding a cell to whole levels each pulse (which would make the law right) was ruled out: the kernel does not round the decilitre away; the check was wrong, not the mod.
- 2026-11-19T23:57:59Z BACKFILL#1050 trap.noted trap:the-law-s-tolerance-was-in-levels-1e v0.1.0: The law's tolerance was in levels (1e-3 level = 10 dL), so a green kernel_fuzz did not mean sub-level volume was conserved; a whole level went red 141/300 but one dL went 0/300.

- 2026-11-19T23:58:18Z BACKFILL#1085 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_TRACE_SLACK_MINIFIED_LOOKUP_ERROR_WITH_FLOWWORKS v0.1.0: Log error 'Tried to get ThingsOfDef of MinifiedThing' with no stack appeared in 2 of 5 scenes runs on tier gimmesomeslack_flowworks, never in eight runs on tier gimmesomeslack; Z_log_budget charges it to GSS. The thrower is not identified.

(recorded on pre-landing 63616d9abf; landed as 6873efad10a1)
- 2026-11-19T23:58:19Z BACKFILL#1088 finding.claimed_fixed finding:SWIM_REAPPLY_FIX_LATE_TOGGLE_STAYS_DRY v0.1.0: HugeThings+FlowWorks: ClearArea spares giants only in NaturalRockOnly; late deep-swim toggle recomputes; settings headers use keys (recorded on pre-landing 601e7482f3; landed as 413f16e88b02)
- 2026-11-19T23:58:21Z BACKFILL#1089 finding.claimed_fixed finding:TRENCH_STRIDE_UNDERPASS_FIX_ONE_BUG v0.1.0: Stride masks per view (deck/floor) compose with the underpass; strider astride a narrow trench steps onto a deck; CanonicalStride cached, DeriveCell alloc-free, cliff-bits test reads production bridged depth; raider ladder-null->fill fallback (unless waiting) and per-square reservation. Earlier minors in 4fa0cab561.
- 2026-11-19T23:58:21Z BACKFILL#1090 trap.noted trap:a-real-no-room-cannot-be-staged-because v0.1.0: A real no-room cannot be staged because the despawned stack's own cell is always free in the search, so the probe uses the seam washPlaceValidator to make the Near search refuse every cell.
